p_{u}={\ell}\tfrac{\mathring{u}}{u^{2}}
from eq.(
3.3
) with
n=-1
, the above leads to solutions for
u_{\tau}
and
p_{u_{\tau}}
given by

\displaystyle u_{\tau}\,=\,(u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega^{2}})\cos\varpi\tau\,+\,\tfrac{1}{\omega}u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}^{2}p_{u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}}\sin{\varpi\tau}\,+\,\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega^{2}}

\displaystyle p_{u_{\tau}}\;=\;\frac{-\omega(u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega})\sin\varpi\tau\,+\,u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}^{2}p_{u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}}\cos\varpi\tau}{\big[(u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega^{2}})\cos\varpi\tau\,+\,\tfrac{1}{\omega}u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}^{2}p_{u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}}\sin{\varpi\tau}\,+\,\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega^{2}}\big]^{2}}

Note that
\varpi\tau=\omega s
and thus the above solutions for
u_{\tau}
and
p_{u_{\tau}}
are equivalent to
u_{s}
and
p_{u_{s}}
given in eq.(
3.4
).

3.5  Summary: Canonical Projective Decomposition for Central Force Motion (with
n=–1
)

It was shown in section
3.4
that, in order to obtain linear equations for
u
with the evolution parameters
s
and
\tau
defined in eq.(
3.3
), we must choose
n=-1
in the point transformation given by eq.(
3.1
). Therefore, we consider a canonical projective decomposition starting with a point transformation given by

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rl}{\text{\footnotesize{point transformation}}}:&\qquad{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}})\;=\;\tfrac{1}{u}{\hat{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\\[10.0pt]
{\text{\footnotesize{subject to}}}:&\qquad\varphi({\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})\;=\;\tfrac{1}{2}(q^{2}-1)\;=\;0\end{array}

The meaning of the new canonical coordinates
({\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},u,{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},p_{u})
is given by eq.(
3.1
):

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{ll}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}({\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})\;=\;{\hat{{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}&\quad,\\[8.0pt]
u({\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})\;=\;\tfrac{1}{r}&\quad,\end{array}

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{ll}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}({\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})\;=\;r({\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}-\dot{r}{\hat{{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}})\;=\;r^{2}\dot{{\hat{{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}}\;=\;{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\times{\hat{{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\\[8.0pt]
p_{u}({\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})\;=\;-r^{2}\dot{r}\end{array}

such that, as per Remark
3.3
,
\{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},{\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},{\hat{{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\}
are the cartesian components of the LVLH basis vectors in an inertial basis with

{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
and
{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
satisfying

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{ll}{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}={\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\times{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\\[4.0pt]
{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}={\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\times{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\end{array}

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{ll}q^{2}=1\\[4.0pt]
p^{2}={\ell}^{2}\end{array}

As per section
3.4
, we consider a particle moving in a potential that is dominated by a central force term,
V^{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}(r)
, of the general form

\displaystyle V({\textbf{{{r}}}},t)\,=\,V^{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}(r)+V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}({\textbf{{{r}}}},t)\;=\;-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{r}-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{2}}{2r^{2}}\,+\,V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}({\textbf{{{r}}}},t)

for some constants
{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}
and
{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{2}
, which may be positive or negative, and where
V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}
is some arbitrary perturbing potential. The Kepler problem for two bodies of mass
m_{a}
and
m_{b}
is given by
{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}=G(m_{a}+m_{b})>0
and
{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{2}=0
.
For the above potential, the Hamiltonian in the
({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},{\bar{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}})
phase space is then given by eq.(
355
) as

\displaystyle{{H}}({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},{\bar{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},t)\;=\;\tfrac{1}{2}u^{2}\big(q^{2}p^{2}\,+\,u^{2}p_{u}^{2}\big)\,-\,{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}u\,-\,\tfrac{1}{2}{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{2}u^{2}\,+\,V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},t)

Such that the equations of motion are given by eq.(
3.2
):

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{ll}\dot{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}\,=\,u^{2}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}&\quad,\\[8.0pt]
\dot{u}\,=\,u^{4}p_{u}&\quad,\end{array}

\displaystyle\qquad\begin{array}[]{ll}\dot{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}\,=\,-u^{2}p^{2}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\,+\,{\vphantom{f}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{f}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\\[8.0pt]
\dot{p}_{u}\,=\,-u\big(p^{2}+2u^{2}p_{u}^{2})+{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}+{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{2}u\,+\,f_{u}\end{array}

where the above have been simplified using
q=1
and
\lambda={\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}=0
, which were proven to be integrals of motion in section
2.4
.
Note that the perturbation terms,
{\vphantom{f}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{f}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
and
f_{u}
, may be written in term of
{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}
— the cartesian components of the total perturbing force (per unit mass) from both conservative and non-conservative perturbations — as seen in eq.(
3.2
):

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lll}{\vphantom{f}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{f}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}&\,:=\,-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}\,+\,{\vphantom{\alphaup}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\alphaup}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}&\;=\,\tfrac{1}{u}({\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\raisebox{0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}\\[8.0pt]
f_{u}&\,:=\,-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial u}\,+\,\alpha_{u}&\;=\;-\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}\end{array}

Transformation of the Evolution Parameter.

We then transform the evolution parameter from time,
t
, to two new parameters,
s
and
\tau
, defined by the differential relations:

\displaystyle\acute{t}\,=\,\tfrac{\mathrm{d}t}{\mathrm{d}s}\;=\;r^{2}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}\qquad,

\displaystyle\mathring{t}\,=\,\tfrac{\mathrm{d}t}{\mathrm{d}\tau}\;=\;\tfrac{r^{2}}{{\ell}}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{u^{2}{\ell}}

(530)

The equations of motion with
s
as the evolution parameter are then given by eq.(
3.3
) as

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{ll}\acute{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}\,=\,{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}&\\[7.0pt]
\acute{u}\,=\,u^{2}p_{u}&\\[7.0pt]
\acute{t}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}&\end{array}

\displaystyle\quad\begin{array}[]{ll}&\acute{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}\,=\,-p^{2}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}+\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}{\vphantom{f}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{f}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\\[6.0pt]
&\acute{p}_{u}\,=\,-\tfrac{1}{u}\big(p^{2}+2u^{2}p_{u}^{2})+\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{u^{2}}+\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{u}+\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}f_{u}\\[6.0pt]
&\acute{p}_{t}\,=\,-\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial t}\,-\,(\tfrac{1}{u}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\,-\,p_{u}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathrm{nc}}}}\end{array}

where
p_{t}=-{{H}}
is equal to the negative of the total energy (per unit mass) and
{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathrm{nc}}}}
is the force per unit mass from non-conservative perturbations. The equations of motion with
\tau
as the evolution parameter are easily found by dividing the above by the angular momentum magnitude,
{\ell}=p
.
The second order equations for
{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
and
u
are then given by eq.(
3.4
) as

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{ll}&\hbox to0.0pt{$\;\,\acute{\vphantom{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\!\acute{\vphantom{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}$\hss}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}+{\ell}^{2}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{u^{3}}\big({\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\raisebox{0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}\\[8.0pt]
&\hbox to0.0pt{$\;\,\acute{\vphantom{u}}\!\acute{\vphantom{u}}$\hss}u\,+\,\omega^{2}u\,-\,{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}\,=\,-\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}\end{array}\qquad,\qquad\omega^{2}:={\ell}^{2}-{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{2}

The second order equations for
{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
and
u
with
\tau
as the evolution parameter are given by eq.(
3.4
) as

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{llll}{\hbox to0.0pt{$\,\mathring{\vphantom{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\mkern 4.5mu\mathring{\vphantom{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}$\hss}}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\,+\,{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{u^{3}{\ell}^{2}}\big({\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\raisebox{0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}-\mathring{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}{\raisebox{0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}\mathring{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}\big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}\;=\;\tfrac{1}{u^{3}{\ell}^{2}}({\hat{{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}{\raisebox{0.6458pt}{$\scriptstyle{\,\otimes\,}$}}{\hat{{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}\\[8.0pt]
{\hbox to0.0pt{$\,\mathring{\vphantom{u}}\mkern 4.5mu\mathring{\vphantom{u}}$\hss}}u\,+\,\varpi^{2}u-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{{\ell}^{2}}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}^{2}u^{3}}\big(u{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}+\mathring{u}\mathring{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}\big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}\end{array}\qquad,\qquad\varpi^{2}:=1-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{2}}{{\ell}^{2}}

where
\mathring{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}={\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}
and
\mathring{u}=u^{2}p_{u}/p
.
In both eq.(
3.5
) and eq.(
3.5
), oscillatory motion in
u
occurs only if
{\ell}^{2}-{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{2}>0
.

Summary: Unperturbed Solution.

For
unperturbed
motion (
{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}={\vphantom{0}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{0}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
), the solution to the above is given in terms of initial conditions by eq.(
3.4
) and eq.(
3.4
):

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rl}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{s}&={\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\cos{\ell}s+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\acute{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\sin{\ell}s\\[5.0pt]
\acute{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}_{s}&=-{\ell}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\sin{\ell}s+\acute{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\cos{\ell}s\;\;={\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{s}\\[5.0pt]
u_{s}&\,=\,(u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega^{2}})\cos\omega s\,+\,\tfrac{1}{\omega}\acute{u}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\sin{\omega s}\,+\,\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega^{2}}\\[5.0pt]
\acute{u}_{s}&\,=\,-\omega(u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega^{2}})\sin\omega s\,+\,\acute{u}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\cos\omega s\end{array}

where we have assumed
{\ell}^{2}-{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{2}>0
such that
\omega
is real.
Using
{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}=\acute{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}
and
p_{u}=\tfrac{\acute{u}}{u^{2}}
, the above leads to

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rl}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{s}&\,=\,{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\cos{\ell}s+\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\sin{\ell}s\\[5.0pt]
{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{s}&\,=\,-{\ell}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\sin{\ell}s+{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\cos{\ell}s\\[5.0pt]
u_{s}&\,=\,(u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega^{2}})\cos\omega s\,+\,\tfrac{1}{\omega}u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}^{2}p_{u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}}\sin{\omega s}\,+\,\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega^{2}}\\[5.0pt]
p_{u_{s}}&\;=\;\frac{-\omega(u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega^{2}})\sin\omega s\,+\,u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}^{2}p_{u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}}\cos\omega s}{\big[(u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega^{2}})\cos\omega s\,+\,\tfrac{1}{\omega}u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}^{2}p_{u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}}\sin{\omega s}\,+\,\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega^{2}}\big]^{2}}\end{array}

Similarly, the solution in terms of
\tau
is given by eq.(
3.4
) and eq.(
3.4
):

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rl}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{\tau}&\,=\,{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\cos\tau+\mathring{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\sin\tau\\[5.0pt]
\mathring{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}_{\tau}&\,=\,-{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\sin\tau+\mathring{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\cos\tau\;\;={\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}_{\tau}\\[5.0pt]
u_{\tau}&\,=\,(u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega^{2}})\cos\varpi\tau\,+\,\tfrac{1}{\varpi}\mathring{u}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\sin{\varpi\tau}\,+\,\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega^{2}}\\[5.0pt]
\mathring{u}_{\tau}&\,=\,-\varpi(u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega^{2}})\sin\varpi\tau\,+\,\mathring{u}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\cos\varpi\tau\end{array}

where we have used
\varpi{\ell}=\omega
and have assumed
\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{2}}{{\ell}^{2}}<1
such that
\varpi
is real.
Then, using
{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}={\ell}\mathring{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}
and
p_{u}={\ell}\tfrac{\mathring{u}}{u^{2}}
, the above leads to

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rl}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{\tau}&\,=\,{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\cos\tau+{\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\sin\tau\\[5.0pt]
{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{\tau}&\,=\,-{\ell}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\sin\tau+{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\cos\tau\\[5.0pt]
u_{\tau}&\,=\,(u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega^{2}})\cos\varpi\tau\,+\,\tfrac{1}{\omega}u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}^{2}p_{u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}}\sin{\varpi\tau}\,+\,\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega^{2}}\\[5.0pt]
p_{u_{\tau}}&\;=\;\frac{-\omega(u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega})\sin\varpi\tau\,+\,u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}^{2}p_{u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}}\cos\varpi\tau}{\big[(u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega^{2}})\cos\varpi\tau\,+\,\tfrac{1}{\omega}u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}^{2}p_{u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}}\sin{\varpi\tau}\,+\,\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}}{\omega^{2}}\big]^{2}}\end{array}

where
\varpi\tau=\omega s
. The solution for the cartesian components of the position and velocity vectors in an inertial basis are then recovered using eq.(
3.1
) and eq.(
3.1
):

\displaystyle{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\;=\;\tfrac{1}{u}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\qquad,\qquad\qquad{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\;=\;u({\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\;-\;up_{u}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})

(590)

where the above has been simplified using the integrals of motion
q=1
and
\lambda={\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}=0
.

4.  REGULARIZATION OF THE TWO-BODY PROBLEM

4.1  The Perturbed Two-Body Problem with Projective Coordinates

This
†
†

Some of the content of this chapter has appeared previously in conference papers by myself (Peterson) , Majji, and Junkins
[
28
,
31
]
. It is published here with their permission.

section will apply the developments of section
3
to the classic problem of two bodies of mass
m_{a}
and
m_{b}
. As this problem is simply a special case of section
3
, we will go through some of the following developments rather quickly.
The dominant central force potential and the Hamiltonian (per unit reduced mass,
m_{r}=\tfrac{m_{a}m_{b}}{m_{a}+m_{b}}
) in the
({\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})
phase space is then given by

\displaystyle V^{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}(r)\,=\,-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}}{r}\qquad,

\displaystyle{{K}}({\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},t)\;=\;\tfrac{1}{2}{\nu}^{2}\,-\,\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}}{r}\,+\,V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}({\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},t)

Where
{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}=G(m_{a}+m_{b})
and where we will treat the perturbing potential,
V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}
, as arbitrary for now but will later examine the
J_{2}
gravitational perturbation as an example.
The above Kepler potential,
V^{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}
, is the form seen in case 1 of eq.(
3.4
). Thus, from the developments of section
3.4
, we now that if we use
n=-1
in the projective decomposition of eq.(
3.1
), then the resulting canonical point transformation from
({\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})\in{\mathbb{R}}^{6}
to
({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},{\bar{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}})\in{\mathbb{R}}^{8}
will lead to equations of motion that are fully linear in the case of unperturbed motion. Therefore, the developments from here forward exactly parallel those of section
3
but for the case
n=-1
and, in section
3.4
, for
{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{2}=0
. We start with a point transformation for the projective decomposition given by

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rl}{\text{\footnotesize{point transformation}}}:&\qquad{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}})\;=\;\tfrac{1}{u}{\hat{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\\[10.0pt]
{\text{\footnotesize{subject to}}}:&\qquad\varphi({\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})\;=\;\tfrac{1}{2}(q^{2}-1)\;=\;0\end{array}

such that the momenta transformation is then given by eq.(
3.1
) as

\displaystyle{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},{\bar{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}})\,=\,\tfrac{u}{q}\big[q^{2}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}-({\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}){\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}-up_{u}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\big]

,

\displaystyle\lambda({\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})\,=\,\tfrac{1}{q^{2}}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}

(597)

Substitution of
{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}})
and
{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},{\bar{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}})
into eq.(
4.1
) leads to the new Hamiltonian as given by eq.(
322
) with
n=-1
:

\displaystyle{{H}}({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},{\bar{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},t)\,=\,\tfrac{1}{2}u^{2}\Big(q^{2}p^{2}-({\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})^{2}\,+\,u^{2}p_{u}^{2}\Big)\,-\,{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}u\,+\,V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},t)

However, as per Remark
3.2
, it was proven in section
2.4
that

q=|{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}|
and
\lambda=\tfrac{1}{q^{2}}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
, are integrals of motion which we may choose to be
1
and
0
, respectively:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{cc}{\text{\footnotesize{integrals}}}\\
{\text{\footnotesize{of motion}}}\end{array}\;:

\displaystyle q\,=\,|{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}|\,=\,1\quad,

\displaystyle{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\,=\,\lambda\,=\,0

Thus, as per Remark
3.5
and eq.(
355
), it follows from the second of the above that the term
({\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})^{2}
may be discarded from the Hamiltonian which simplifies to

\displaystyle{{H}}({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},{\bar{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},t)\;=\;\tfrac{1}{2}u^{2}\big(q^{2}p^{2}\,+\,u^{2}p_{u}^{2}\big)\,-\,{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}u\,+\,V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},t)

The canonical equations of motion for
{\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}
and
{\bar{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}
, including non-conservative perturbations, are then obtained from
{{H}}
using eq.(
265
). This leads to eq.(
3.2
) with
n=-1
:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{ll}\dot{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}\,=\,u^{2}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}&\quad,\\[8.0pt]
\dot{u}\,=\,u^{4}p_{u}&\quad,\end{array}

\displaystyle\qquad\begin{array}[]{ll}\dot{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}\,=\,-u^{2}p^{2}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\,-\,\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}\,+\,{\vphantom{\alphaup}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\alphaup}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\\[8.0pt]
\dot{p}_{u}\,=\,-u\big(p^{2}+2u^{2}p_{u}^{2})+{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}\,-\,\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial u}\,+\,\alpha_{u}\end{array}

where, after differentiating
{{H}}
, the above have been simplified using
q=1
.
For some known solutions for
{\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}
and
{\bar{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}
, the cartesian position and velocity components,
{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
and
{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
, may then be recovered using eq.(
4.1
) and eq.(
597
). Note that, although not necessary, these relations may be simplified using the constants
q=1
and
{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}=0
leading to

\displaystyle{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\;=\;\tfrac{1}{u}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\qquad,

\displaystyle{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}

\displaystyle\;=\;u({\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}-up_{u}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})

(609)

It can quickly be verified from eq.(
4.1
) and eq.(
609
) that the above
{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
is equivalent to
{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}=\dot{{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}
expressed in terms of the canonical coordinates.
Note that, although the above are correct relations between
({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},{\bar{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}})
and
({\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})
, they should
not
be used when forming the Hamiltonian.
40
40

40

The Hamiltonian should be formed using the full unsimplified expressions
{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}})=\tfrac{1}{u{\lvert{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\rvert}}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
and
{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},{\bar{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}})=\tfrac{u}{q}\big[q^{2}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}-({\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}){\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}-up_{u}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\big]
.

Meaning of the Canonical Projective Coordinates & Relation to Orbital Reference Frames.

The meaning of the canonical coordinates
({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},{\bar{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}})
is given by eq.(
3.1
):

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{ll}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\;=\;{\hat{{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}&\quad,\\[8.0pt]
u\;=\;\tfrac{1}{r}&\quad,\end{array}

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{ll}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\;=\;-\tfrac{1}{r}{{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\times}}}{{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\times}}}{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\,=\,r({\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}-\dot{r}{\hat{{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}})\;=\;{{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\times}}}{\hat{{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\;=\;r^{2}\dot{{\hat{{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}}\\[8.0pt]
p_{u}\;=\;-r^{2}\dot{r}\end{array}

where
{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
is the inertial cartesian coordinate vector of the specific angular momentum. As per Remark
3.3
and eq.(
3.3
),
{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
and
{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
are orthogonal and directly define the local vertical, local horizontal (LVLH) basis:

\displaystyle\{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},{\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},{\hat{{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\}\,=\,\{{\hat{{\vphantom{e}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{e}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}_{r},{\hat{{\vphantom{e}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{e}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}_{\scriptscriptstyle{\theta}},{\hat{{\vphantom{e}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{e}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}_{{\ell}}\}\;=\;\begin{array}[]{cc}{\text{\footnotesize{LVLH}}}\\
{\text{\footnotesize{basis}}}\end{array}

,

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lll}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}=\tfrac{1}{{\ell}^{2}}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\times{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}&\!\!\!\equiv\;{\hat{{\vphantom{e}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{e}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}_{r}\\[4.0pt]
{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}={\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\times{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}&\!\!\!\equiv\;{\ell}{\hat{{\vphantom{e}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{e}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}_{\scriptscriptstyle{\theta}}\\[4.0pt]
{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}={\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\times{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}&\!\!\!\equiv\;{\ell}{\hat{{\vphantom{e}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{e}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}_{\ell}\end{array}

,

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{ll}q^{2}=1\\[4.0pt]
p^{2}={\ell}^{2}\end{array}

Let us consider another set of three mutually orthogonal vectors which are often encountered in the study of the classic 2BP; the Laplace-Runge-Lenz (LRL) vector/eccentricity vector,
{\boldsymbol{e}}
, and the Hamilton vector,
{\boldsymbol{k}}
, along with the specific angular momentum vector,
{\bm{\ell}}
. It is well-known that these three vectors are conserved for Keplerian motion and are defined in terms of the position and velocity vectors as
41
41

41

The Laplace-Runge-Lenz (LRL) vector and the Hamilton vector are often defined (per unit reduced mass) as
{\boldsymbol{A}}=\dot{{\boldsymbol{r}}}\times{\bm{\ell}}-{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}{\mkern 1.5mu\hat{\mkern-1.5mu{\boldsymbol{r}}}}
and
{\boldsymbol{K}}=\tfrac{1}{{\ell}^{2}}{\bm{\ell}}\times{\boldsymbol{A}}
, respectively. The eccentricity vector,
{\boldsymbol{e}}
, and the vector
{\boldsymbol{k}}
seen in eq.(
4.1
) are simply a scaling of
{\boldsymbol{A}}
and
{\boldsymbol{K}}
by

–

\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}

.

\displaystyle{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}{\boldsymbol{e}}=\dot{{\boldsymbol{r}}}\times{\bm{\ell}}-{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}{\mkern 1.5mu\hat{\mkern-1.5mu{\boldsymbol{r}}}}\quad,

\displaystyle{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}{\boldsymbol{k}}=\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}}{{\ell}^{2}}({\bm{\ell}}\times{\boldsymbol{e}})\,=\,\dot{{\boldsymbol{r}}}-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}}{{\ell}^{2}}{\bm{\ell}}\times{\mkern 1.5mu\hat{\mkern-1.5mu{\boldsymbol{r}}}}\quad,

\displaystyle{\bm{\ell}}={\boldsymbol{r}}\times\dot{{\boldsymbol{r}}}

The normalization of these vectors,
\{{\mkern 1.5mu\hat{\mkern-1.5mu{\boldsymbol{e}}}},{\mkern 1.5mu\hat{\mkern-1.5mu{\boldsymbol{k}}}},\hat{\bm{\ell}}\}
, defines an orthonormal basis often called the
perifocal basis
. For Keplerian motion, this basis is inertial/stationary with
{\mkern 1.5mu\hat{\mkern-1.5mu{\boldsymbol{e}}}}
and
{\mkern 1.5mu\hat{\mkern-1.5mu{\boldsymbol{k}}}}
defining the orbital plane;
{\mkern 1.5mu\hat{\mkern-1.5mu{\boldsymbol{e}}}}
directed towards periapse,
{\mkern 1.5mu\hat{\mkern-1.5mu{\boldsymbol{k}}}}
directed along the velocity at periapse (direction tangent to the orbit), and
\hat{\bm{\ell}}
directed normal to the orbit plane, completing the right-handed triad. The magnitude
e={\lvert{\boldsymbol{e}}\rvert}
is the usual dimensionless eccentricity of the orbit.
The cartesian coordinate representation of the above are given in terms of
({\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})
and in terms of
({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},{\bar{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}})
as

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rlllllll}{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.09999pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}{\vphantom{e}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{e}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}&\!\!\!\!=\,{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\times{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}-{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.09999pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}{\hat{{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\,=\,-({\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\times}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{\times}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}+{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.09999pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}{\hat{{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}})&\!\!=\,(u{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}-u^{2}p_{u}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})\times{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\,-\,{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}&\!\!\!\!=\,p^{2}(u-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.16998pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}}{p^{2}}){\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\,+\,u^{2}p_{u}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\\[4.0pt]
{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.09999pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}{\vphantom{k}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{k}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}&\!\!\!\!=\,\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.16998pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}}{{\ell}^{2}}({\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\times{\vphantom{e}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{e}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})\,=\,{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.16998pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}}{{\ell}^{2}}{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\times{\hat{{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}&\!\!=\;(u{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}-u^{2}p_{u}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})\,-\,\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.16998pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}}{p^{2}}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}&\!\!\!\!=\,(u-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.16998pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}}{p^{2}}){\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\,-\,u^{2}p_{u}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\\[4.0pt]
{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}&\!\!\!\!=\,{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\times{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}&\!\!=\;{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\times{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\end{array}

where the above expressions for
{\vphantom{e}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{e}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
and
{\vphantom{k}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{k}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
are simplified using
q=1
and
{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}=0
.

The Perturbation Terms.

The perturbing terms in eq.(
4.1
) — the terms involving
V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}
and the generalized forces
{\vphantom{\alphaup}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\alphaup}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
and
\alpha_{u}
— can be written in terms of the inertial components of the conservative and non-conservative perturbing forces (per unit reduced mass) — given by
-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}
and
{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathrm{nc}}}}
, respectively — using eq.(
3.2
):

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{rl}\begin{array}[]{cc}{\text{\footnotesize{conservative}}}\\
{\text{\footnotesize{perturbations}}}\end{array}&\left\{\hskip 9.24994pt\begin{array}[]{rll}\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}&=\;\tfrac{\uppartial{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}^{{\scriptscriptstyle{\textsf{{T}}}}}\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}&\;=\;\tfrac{1}{u}\big[\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}\,-\,(\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}){\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\big]\\[6.0pt]
\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial u}&=\;\tfrac{\uppartial{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}{\uppartial u}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}&\;=\;-\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\end{array}\right.\\[20.0pt]
\begin{array}[]{cc}{\text{\footnotesize{non-conservative}}}\\
{\text{\footnotesize{perturbations}}}\end{array}&\left\{\hskip 9.24994pt\;\begin{array}[]{rll}{\vphantom{\alphaup}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\alphaup}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}&=\;\tfrac{\uppartial{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}^{{\scriptscriptstyle{\textsf{{T}}}}}{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathrm{nc}}}}&\;=\;\tfrac{1}{u}\big[{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathrm{nc}}}}\;-\;({\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathrm{nc}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}){\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\big]\\[6.0pt]
\alpha_{u}&=\;\tfrac{\uppartial{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}{\uppartial u}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathrm{nc}}}}&\;=\;-\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathrm{nc}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\end{array}\right.\end{array}

Where
\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}=\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial r}
.
If we define
{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}
as in eq.(
3.2
) to be the total perturbing force, then the above may be combined as in equation
eq.(
3.2
):

\displaystyle\left.\begin{array}[]{lll}-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}+{\vphantom{\alphaup}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\alphaup}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}&\,=\,\tfrac{1}{u}\big[{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}-({\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}){\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\big]\\[8.0pt]
-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial u}+\alpha_{u}&\,=\,-\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\end{array}\right\}\;\;{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}

\displaystyle\,:=\,-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}+{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathrm{nc}}}}

where
{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
is simply the radial component of
{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}
. These perturbations govern the evolution of the angular momentum which, from eq.(
3.6
), is given by

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{ll}{\dot{{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{{\ell}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\;=\;{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\times(-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}\,+\,{\vphantom{\alphaup}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\alphaup}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})\;=\;\tfrac{1}{u}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\times{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}\;=\;\tfrac{1}{u}\big[({\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}){\hat{{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\,-\,({\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}){\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\big]\\[6.0pt]
\dot{{\ell}}\;=\;\dot{p}\;=\;(-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}\,+\,{\vphantom{\alphaup}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\alphaup}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}=\,\tfrac{1}{u}{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\end{array}

4.2  Transformation of the Evolution Parameter & Linearization

Following the developments of section
3.3
,
we now use the extended phase space to transform the evolution parameter from time,
t
, to two new parameters,
s
and
\tau
, defined by the differential relations in eq.(
3.3
):

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mathrm{d}t}{\mathrm{d}s}\;=\;r^{2}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}\qquad,

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mathrm{d}t}{\mathrm{d}\tau}\;=\;\tfrac{r^{2}}{{\ell}}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{u^{2}{\ell}}

(662)

Note that
\tau
is the true anomaly (or the change in the true anomaly,
\Delta\tau
, depending on initial conditions).
The extended Hamiltonian for
s
, denoted
\widetilde{{H}}
, is then given as seen in eq.(
3.3
):

\displaystyle\widetilde{{H}}\;=\;\tfrac{\mathrm{d}t}{\mathrm{d}s}({{H}}+p_{t})\;=\;\tfrac{1}{2}\big(q^{2}p^{2}\,+\,u^{2}p_{u}^{2}\big)\,-\,\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}}{u}\,+\,\widetilde{V}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},t)\,+\,\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}p_{t}

where
\widetilde{V}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}=\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}
.
The equations of motion with
s
as the evolution parameter are then
found from eq.(
3.3
) and eq.(
3.3
) with
n=-1
:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{ll}\acute{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}\,=\,{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}&\\[7.0pt]
\acute{u}\,=\,u^{2}p_{u}&\\[7.0pt]
\acute{t}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}&\end{array}

\displaystyle\quad\begin{array}[]{ll}\acute{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}&\,=\,-p^{2}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}+\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}(-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}+{\vphantom{\alphaup}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\alphaup}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})\\[6.0pt]
\acute{p}_{u}&\,=\,-up_{u}^{2}-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}}{u^{2}}-\tfrac{\uppartial\widetilde{V}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial u}+\tfrac{2}{u^{3}}p_{t}+\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}\alpha_{u}\\[6.0pt]
\acute{p}_{t}&\,=\,-\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}(\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial t}+\alpha_{t})\,=\,-\tfrac{\uppartial\widetilde{V}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial t}-{\vphantom{\alphaup}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\alphaup}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}-u^{2}p_{u}\alpha_{u}\end{array}

where we have used
\acute{t}\alpha_{t}=-({\vphantom{\alphaup}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\alphaup}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}\acute{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}+\alpha_{u}\acute{u})
in the above equation for
\acute{p}_{t}
.
As seen in eq.(
399
) through eq.(
407
), the extended Hamiltonian with
\tau
as the evolution parameter is given simply by
\widetilde{{M}}=\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}\widetilde{{H}}
and equations of motion are most easily obtained simply by dividing eq.(
4.2
) by
{\ell}=p
:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{ll}\mathring{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}\,=\,{\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}&\\[7.0pt]
\mathring{u}\,=\,u^{2}\tfrac{p_{u}}{p}&\\[7.0pt]
\mathring{t}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{pu^{2}}&\end{array}

\displaystyle\quad\begin{array}[]{ll}&\mathring{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}\,=\,-p{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}+\tfrac{1}{pu^{2}}(-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}+{\vphantom{\alphaup}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\alphaup}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})\\[6.0pt]
&\mathring{p}_{u}\,=\,-\tfrac{1}{p}\big[up_{u}^{2}+\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}}{u^{2}}+\tfrac{\uppartial\widetilde{V}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial u}-\tfrac{2}{u^{3}}p_{t}-\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}\alpha_{u}\big]\\[6.0pt]
&\mathring{p}_{t}\,=\,-\tfrac{1}{pu^{2}}\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial t}\,-\,{\vphantom{\alphaup}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\alphaup}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\,-\,u^{2}\tfrac{p_{u}}{p}\alpha_{u}\end{array}

As seen in eq.(
962
) of appendix
B
,
p_{t}=-{{H}}
which gives an additional relation between the extended phase space coordinates:

\displaystyle p_{t}\,=\,-\tfrac{1}{2}u^{2}\big(p^{2}\,+\,u^{2}p_{u}^{2}\big)\,+\,{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}u\,-\,V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}

For the problem at hand, the above means that
p_{t}
is equal to the negative of the total energy (per unit reduced mass) and is an integral of motion if
\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial t}=0
and if no non-conservative forces do work on the system .
The above can be used to re-write the equations of motion for
\acute{p}_{u}
and
\mathring{p}_{u}
as

\displaystyle\acute{p}_{u}\,=\,-\tfrac{1}{u}(p^{2}+2u^{2}p_{u}^{2})+\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}}{u^{2}}+\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}(-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial u}+\alpha_{u})\qquad,

\displaystyle\mathring{p}_{u}\,=\,\frac{\acute{p}_{u}}{p}

Lastly, note from eq.(
609
), eq.(
4.2
), and eq.(
4.2
) that, for some known
{\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}
and
{\bar{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}
, we may recover
{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
and
{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
using

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{ll}&{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{u}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\\[4.0pt]
&{\vphantom{v}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{v}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\,=\,u{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}-u^{2}p_{u}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}(u\dot{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}-\dot{u}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})\,=\,u\acute{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}-\acute{u}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\,=\,p(u\mathring{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}-\mathring{u}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})\end{array}

\,\scriptscriptstyle{\blacksquare}\,
Remark 4.1
.

The equations of motion for the canonical coordinates
({\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},u,{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},p_{u})
with
t
,
s
, and
\tau
(true anomaly) as the evolution parameter are given by eq.(
4.1
), eq.(
4.2
), and eq.(
4.2
), respectively (where the latter two also include equations for the extended phase space coordinates,
t
and
p_{t}
). The equations are non-singular for all eccentricities and inclinations. The equations with
t
and
s
as the evolution parameter are also non-singular for rectilinear motion for which
{\ell}=p=0
. However the equations with
\tau
as the evolution parameter exhibit singularities for rectilinear motion as they contain terms of the form
\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}
.

4.2.1  Second Order Equations for a Perturbed Oscillator

As shown in sections
3.4
and
3.4
, eq.(
4.2
) through eq.(
4.2
) can be used to obtain second order equations for
{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
and
u
that are linear for unperturbed motion. These are given by eq.(
3.4
) with
n=-1
and eq.(
3.4
) with
{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{1}={\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}
and
{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}_{2}=0
. With
s
as the evolution parameter, this leads to

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mathrm{d}^{2}{{\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}}}{\mathrm{d}{s}^{2}}\;\;\left\{\;\;\begin{array}[]{llll}&\hbox to0.0pt{$\;\,\acute{\vphantom{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\!\acute{\vphantom{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}$\hss}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}+{\ell}^{2}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}(-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}+{\vphantom{\alphaup}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\alphaup}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})&\!=\,\;\tfrac{1}{u^{3}}\big({\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\otimes{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}\\[8.0pt]
&\hbox to0.0pt{$\;\,\acute{\vphantom{u}}\!\acute{\vphantom{u}}$\hss}u\,+\,{\ell}^{2}u\,-\,{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}\,=\,-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial u}+\alpha_{u}&\!=\,\;-\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}\end{array}\right.

where
{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}=-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{r}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{r}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}+{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scriptscriptstyle{{\mathrm{nc}}}}
is the inertial cartesian coordinate vector of the total perturbing force (per unit reduced mass).
With
\tau
as the evolution parameter:

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mathrm{d}^{2}{{\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}}}{\mathrm{d}{\tau}^{2}}\;\;\left\{\;\;\begin{array}[]{llllll}&\accentset{\circ\circ}{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}\,+\,{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}^{2}u^{2}}\big({\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\otimes{\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}\big(-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}+{\vphantom{\alphaup}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\alphaup}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\big)&\!=\,\;\tfrac{1}{u^{3}{\ell}^{2}}({\hat{{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\otimes{\hat{{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}\\[8.0pt]
&\accentset{\circ\circ}{u}\,+\,u-\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}}{{\ell}^{2}}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}^{2}}\Big(-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial u}+\alpha_{u}\,-\,\tfrac{\mathring{u}}{u^{2}}{\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}(-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}+{\vphantom{\alphaup}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\alphaup}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}})\Big)&\!=\,\;\tfrac{1}{u^{3}{\ell}^{2}}\big(u{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}+\mathring{u}{\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\big){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}\end{array}\right.

where the
\accentset{\circ\circ}{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}
equation has used
{\hat{{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\otimes{\hat{{\vphantom{{\ell}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{\ell}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}={\text{1}}\hskip-0.56905pt_{3}-{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\otimes{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}-{\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\otimes{\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}
, and where
we have written
{\ell}^{2}
instead of
p^{2}
simply to remind us that this is the angular momentum magnitude which is constant for unperturbed motion. For perturbed motion, the evolution of
{\ell}=p=|\acute{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}|
is governed by eq.(
4.1
) which leads to

\displaystyle{\ell}=p=|\acute{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}|\;\;:

\displaystyle\acute{{\ell}}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{u^{2}}(-\tfrac{\uppartial V^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}}{\uppartial{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}+{\vphantom{\alphaup}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\alphaup}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}){\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\,=\,\tfrac{1}{u^{3}}{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}{\mkern 1.5mu\cdot\mkern 1.5mu}{\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}\qquad,

\displaystyle\mathring{{\ell}}\,=\,\tfrac{\acute{{\ell}}}{{\ell}}

(691)

4.2.2  Closed-Form Solutions for the Unperturbed Problem

For
unperturbed
motion (
{\vphantom{a}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{a}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}^{\scalebox{0.7}{$1$}}={\vphantom{0}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{0}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
), the right-hand-side of eq.(
4.2.1
) through eq.(
4.2.1
) vanishes and the second order equations of motion with either
s
or
\tau
as the evolution parameter are of the general form

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mathrm{d}^{2}{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}{\mathrm{d}{{\varepsilon}}^{2}}\;+\;\omega^{2}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}\;=\;0\quad,

\displaystyle\tfrac{\mathrm{d}^{2}{u}}{\mathrm{d}{{\varepsilon}}^{2}}\;+\;\omega^{2}u\;-\;b\;=\;0

(692)

where, depending on whether
{\varepsilon}=s
or
{\varepsilon}=\tau
, the constants
\omega
and
b
are given by

\displaystyle{\text{\footnotesize{if }}}\,{\varepsilon}\,=\,s\;\;\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\omega\,=\,{\ell}\,=\,p\\[4.0pt]
b\,=\,{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{3.44444pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}\end{array}\right.

,

\displaystyle{\text{\footnotesize{if }}}\,{\varepsilon}\,=\,\tau\;\;\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\omega\,=\,1\\[4.0pt]
b\,=\,\tfrac{{\mkern-4.0mu\textnormal{\vtop{\halign{#\cr\raisebox{2.41112pt}{\scriptsize{--}}\cr$\mkern 4.0mu{\mathrm{k}}$\cr}}}\vphantom{k}}}{{\ell}^{2}}\end{array}\right.

where
{\ell}=p=|\acute{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}|
is the specific angular momentum magnitude, which is constant for unperturbed motion. eq.(
692
) is then easily solved to obtain closed-form solutions for for
{\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}
and
\tfrac{\mathrm{d}{\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}}{\mathrm{d}{\varepsilon}}
in terms of initial conditions and either
s
or
\tau
:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{ll}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{\varepsilon}}={\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\cos\omega{\varepsilon}+\tfrac{1}{\omega}(\tfrac{\mathrm{d}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}{\mathrm{d}{\varepsilon}})_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\sin\omega{\varepsilon}\\[6.0pt]
u_{{\varepsilon}}=(u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}-\tfrac{b}{\omega^{2}})\cos\omega{\varepsilon}+\tfrac{1}{\omega}(\tfrac{\mathrm{d}u}{\mathrm{d}{\varepsilon}})_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\sin\omega{\varepsilon}\,+\,\tfrac{b}{\omega^{2}}\\[8.0pt]
\tfrac{\mathrm{d}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{\varepsilon}}}{\mathrm{d}{\varepsilon}}=-\omega{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\sin\omega{\varepsilon}+(\tfrac{\mathrm{d}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}{\mathrm{d}{\varepsilon}})_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\cos\omega{\varepsilon}\\[6.0pt]
\tfrac{\mathrm{d}u_{{\varepsilon}}}{\mathrm{d}{\varepsilon}}=-\omega(u_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}-\tfrac{b}{\omega^{2}})\sin\omega{\varepsilon}+(\tfrac{\mathrm{d}u}{\mathrm{d}{\varepsilon}})_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\cos\omega{\varepsilon}\end{array}

We will use the above to construct closed-form solutions for the following eight-coordinate sets of state space coordinates:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lllllll}{\vphantom{z}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{z}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}:=\;({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},{\bar{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}})&\!\!\!=\,({\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},u,{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},p_{u})\\[8.0pt]
{\vphantom{\upxi}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\upxi}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}:=\;({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},\tfrac{\mathrm{d}{\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}}{\mathrm{d}s})&\!\!\!=\,({\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},u,{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},w)&\quad,\qquad\;w\,:=\,u^{2}p_{u}\,=\,\acute{u}\,=\,-\dot{r}\\[8.0pt]
{\vphantom{{{\text{\textchi}}}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{{\text{\textchi}}}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}:=\;({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},\tfrac{\mathrm{d}{\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}}{\mathrm{d}\tau})&\!\!\!=\,({\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}},u,{\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},\tilde{w})&\quad,\qquad\tilde{w}:=\,\tfrac{u^{2}}{p}p_{u}\,=\,\mathring{u}\,=\,-\dot{r}/{\ell}\end{array}

where we have used the fact that
\acute{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}={\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
and
\mathring{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}={\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}
and where, for greater clarity in subsequent developments, we have reassigned
\acute{u}
and
\mathring{u}
the names
w
and
\tilde{w}
.

\,\scriptscriptstyle{\blacksquare}\,
Remark 4.2
.

Unlike
{\vphantom{\upxi}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\upxi}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
and
{\vphantom{z}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{z}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
, the set of coordinates
{\vphantom{{{\text{\textchi}}}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{{\text{\textchi}}}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
defined in eq.(
4.2.2
) does not actually constitute a complete state space; it contains
{\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}
rather than
{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
. The velocity, or any parameter that is a function of velocity (such as
{\ell}
) is not fully defined in terms of
{\hat{{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}
.
If we were to use
{\vphantom{{{\text{\textchi}}}}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{{{\text{\textchi}}}}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}
as a “state space”, we would need to include
{\ell}
as an additional coordinate (or some other variable that fully defines
{\ell}
).

Velocity Phase Space Solution for
({\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}},\tfrac{\mathrm{d}{\bar{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}}}{\mathrm{d}{\varepsilon}})
.

With
\omega
and
b
defined in eq.(
4.2.2
), eq.(
4.2.2
) with
{\varepsilon}=s
leads to

\displaystyle{\vphantom{\upxi}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\upxi}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}({\vphantom{\upxi}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{\upxi}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}},s)\;\;\left\{\;\;\begin{array}[]{rlll}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{s}&\,=\,{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\cos{\ell}s\,+\,\tfrac{1}{{\ell}}{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\sin{\ell}s\\[6.0pt]
{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{s}&\,=\,-{\ell}{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\sin{\ell}s\,+\,{\vphantom{p}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{p}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}_{{{\scalebox{0.7}{$0$}}}}\cos{\ell}s&\quad=\acute{{\vphantom{q}{\underline{\smash{\mathrm{q}}\mkern-1.0mu}{\mkern 1.0mu}}}}\\[6.0pt]
